(function() {
    // Block script creation
    var originalCreateElement = document.createElement;
    document.createElement = function(tagName) {
        var element = originalCreateElement.call(this, tagName);
        if (tagName.toLowerCase() === 'script') {
            var originalSrcSetter = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLScriptElement.prototype, 'src').set;
            Object.defineProperty(element, 'src', {
                set: function(value) {
                    if (value && value.includes('webfont')) {
                        console.log('BLOCKED webfont script:', value);
                        return;
                    }
                    originalSrcSetter.call(this, value);
                },
                get: function() {
                    return this.getAttribute('src');
                }
            });
        }
        return element;
    };

    // Block appendChild/insertBefore
    var originalAppendChild = Node.prototype.appendChild;
    var originalInsertBefore = Node.prototype.insertBefore;

        if (child.tagName === 'SCRIPT' && child.src && child.src.includes('webfont')) {
            console.log('BLOCKED appendChild webfont');
            return child;
        }
        return originalAppendChild.call(this, child);
    };
    
    Node.prototype.insertBefore = function(child, before) {
        if (child.tagName === 'SCRIPT' && child.src && child.src.includes('webfont')) {
            console.log('BLOCKED insertBefore webfont');
            return child;
        }
        return originalInsertBefore.call(this, child, before);
    };

    window.WebFont = { load: function() { console.log('WebFont.load BLOCKED'); } };

      content: 'site-links-sub-mob-jew',
      icon: 'code-icon-58-jew'
    },
    {
      trigger: 'heading-menu-drop-mob-sub-price',
      content: 'site-links-sub-mob-price',
      icon: 'code-icon-58-price'
    },
    {
      trigger: 'heading-menu-drop-mob-sub-help',
      content: 'site-links-sub-mob-help',
      icon: 'code-icon-58-help'
    },
    {
      trigger: 'heading-menu-drop-mob-sub-bul',
      content: 'site-links-sub-mob-bul',
      icon: 'code-icon-58-bul'
    },
    {
      trigger: 'heading-menu-drop-mob-sub-jew2',
      content: 'site-links-sub-mob-jew2',
      icon: 'code-icon-58-jew2'
    }
  ];
  
  // Initialize each dropdown
  dropdowns.forEach(function(dropdown) {
    initializeDropdown(
      document.getElementById(dropdown.trigger),
      document.getElementById(dropdown.content),
      document.getElementById(dropdown.icon)
    );
  });
  
  // Function to initialize dropdown behavior
  function initializeDropdown(menuTrigger, submenu, icon) {
    // Skip if any element doesn't exist
    if (!menuTrigger || !submenu || !icon) {
      console.warn('Missing element for dropdown', menuTrigger, submenu, icon);
      return;
    }
    
    // Set initial state
    submenu.style.height = '0px';
    submenu.style.opacity = '0';
    submenu.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    submenu.style.transition = 'height 0.3s ease, opacity 0.3s ease';
    icon.style.transition = 'transform 0.3s ease';
    
    // Track if submenu is open or closed
    let isOpen = false;
    
    // Function to toggle submenu
    function toggleSubmenu(event) {
      event.preventDefault();
      
      if (!isOpen) {
        // First set height to auto to calculate actual height
        submenu.style.height = 'auto';
        const height = submenu.offsetHeight + 'px';
        
        // Reset to closed state before animating
        submenu.style.height = '0px';
        
        // Force browser to recognize the change before animating
        setTimeout(function() {
          submenu.style.height = height;
          submenu.style.opacity = '1';
          icon.style.transform = 'rotateX(180deg)';
        }, 10);
        
        isOpen = true;
      } else {
        submenu.style.height = '0px';
        submenu.style.opacity = '0';
        icon.style.transform = 'rotateX(0deg)';
        isOpen = false;
      }
    }
    
    // Add click event listener
    menuTrigger.addEventListener('click', toggleSubmenu);
  }
});

</script></div></div><a href="/contact-us" class="navbar1_link-contactus w-nav-link">Contact Us</a><div class="shopping-cart"><a href="/app/cart" target="_blank" class="link-block-55 w-inline-block"><div class="text-block-1387">Cart</div><div id="cart-time-text" class="cart-time-text"></div><div class="cart-icon-box"><div id="cart-svg" class="cart-svg w-embed"><?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>

</svg></div></div><div class="rich-text-block-2 w-richtext"><p><strong>Which is Better -  Gold or Silver?</strong></p></div></div><div style="width:100%;height:0px" class="faq4_answer"><div class="margin-bottom margin-small"><div class="w-richtext"><p>Many investors choose a combination of both gold and silver to balance stability with growth potential:<strong>‍</strong></p><p>‍</p><p><strong>Gold</strong> if you want long-term stability, wealth preservation, and lower price swings.</p><p>‍<strong>Silver </strong>if you want affordability, potential for higher percentage returns, and exposure to industrial demand.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="collection-item-4 w-dyn-item"><div class="faq4_accordion"><div data-w-id="dcd4373d-5b1c-726d-729f-09ffae9cd783" class="faq4_question"><div class="faq4_icon-wrappper"><div class="icon-embed-small w-embed"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 32 32" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">

Provide high liquidity, making them easy to buy and sell globally

What is &#x27;Investment Selection&#x27;?</p></div></div><div style="width:100%;height:0px" class="faq4_answer"><div class="margin-bottom margin-small"><div class="w-richtext">Our Investment Selection bars and coins are brand-agnostic options designed to give you the lowest possible premium for the weight you’re buying. Our products are ≥ 99.9% pure (many gold coins/bars are 99.99%) from recognised mints. The item’s fine weight and purity are stamped or certified by the mint, and products are delivered in standard investment formats (coins, minted bars, or cast bars).</div></div></div></div></div><div role="listitem" class="collection-item-4 w-dyn-item"><div class="faq4_accordion"><div data-w-id="dcd4373d-5b1c-726d-729f-09ffae9cd783" class="faq4_question"><div class="faq4_icon-wrappper"><div class="icon-embed-small w-embed"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 32 32" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
<path d="M25.3333 15.667V16.3336C25.3333 16.7018 25.0349 17.0003 24.6667 17.0003H17V24.667C17 25.0351 16.7015 25.3336 16.3333 25.3336H15.6667C15.2985 25.3336 15 25.0351 15 24.667V17.0003H7.3333C6.96511 17.0003 6.66663 16.7018 6.66663 16.3336V15.667C6.66663 15.2988 6.96511 15.0003 7.3333 15.0003H15V7.33365C15 6.96546 15.2985 6.66699 15.6667 6.66699H16.3333C16.7015 6.66699 17 6.96546 17 7.33365V15.0003H24.6667C25.0349 15.0003 25.3333 15.2988 25.3333 15.667Z" fill="currentColor"/>
</svg></div></div><div class="rich-text-block-2 w-richtext">What is the spot (live) price?</div></div><div style="width:100%;height:0px" class="faq4_answer"><div class="margin-bottom margin-small"><div class="w-richtext"><p>The spot price is the real-time wholesale market price for unallocated, unfabricated gold or silver. It changes throughout the day with supply, demand, and macro events. Retail products are priced at spot + premium. The premium covers refining, minting, assay/packaging, transport, insurance, and dealer costs—and it varies by product, brand, and market conditions. (When you sell back to us, the buyback price is typically spot minus a small spread.)The spot price, or live price, refers to the current market price at which gold, silver, or other commodities can be bought or sold for immediate delivery. It fluctuates constantly based on supply and demand, geopolitical events, and economic factors.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="margin-top margin-xxlarge"><div class="text-align-center"><div class="max-width-medium"><div class="margin-bottom margin-xsmall"><div class="text-block-1273">Still have questions?</div></div><p class="text-size-medium-copy-copy">See all of our FAQs</p><div class="margin-top margin-medium"><div class="button-group-copy"><a href="/faqs" class="button is-secondary w-button">All FAQs</a></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></section><section class="section_testimonial11"><div class="padding-global"><div class="container-large"><div class="padding-section-large"><div class="testimonial11_component"><div class="margin-bottom margin-xxlarge"><div class="max-width-large"><div class="margin-bottom margin-small"><h2 id="custom-testimonials" class="heading-31">Customer Testimonials</h2></div><p class="text-size-medium">Trusted by Kiwi&#x27;s in Christchurch and throughout NZ.</p></div><div class="user-reviews-cms-home w-dyn-list"><div role="list" class="w-dyn-items"><div role="listitem" class="w-dyn-item"><div id="user-review-0001" class="user-review-block"><div class="review-head"><div class="review-name">Mitchell Carver</div><div class="cust-icon-wrap"><div class="text-block-1330">Customer testimonial</div></div></div><div id="stars-wrapper" class="stars-wrapper"><div id="star1" class="icon-embed-xxsmall w-embed"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">

// Wait for the DOM to be fully loaded
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  // Find the div element with the ID 'telegram-button'
  var telegramButton = document.getElementById('telegram-button');

  // Check if the element exists
  if (telegramButton) {
    // Add a click event listener to the div
    telegramButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
      // Find the hidden input field and the real submit button
      var telegramSentInput = document.getElementById('telegram-sent');
      var submitButton = document.getElementById('contact-pop-button');

      // Populate the hidden input field with the value 'true'
      if (telegramSentInput) {
        telegramSentInput.value = 'true';
      }

      // Trigger the click of the actual form submit button
      if (submitButton) {
        submitButton.click();
      }

      // Get the message for the Telegram link
      var chatMessageInput = document.getElementById('chat-message');
      var message = '';

      if (chatMessageInput) {
        message = encodeURIComponent(chatMessageInput.value);
      }

      var url = 'https://t.me/Christchurch_Gold?text=' + message;

      // --- MODIFIED LINE ---
      // Define the window features for the popup
      var windowFeatures = 'width=600,height=1024,scrollbars=yes,resizable=yes';
      
      // Open the specified URL in a new, resized window
      window.open(url, '_blank', windowFeatures);
    });

    // Optional: Change the cursor to a pointer on hover to indicate it's clickable
    telegramButton.style.cursor = 'pointer';
  }
});
</script></div><div class="code-populate-first-last-name w-embed w-script"><script>
// Wait for the DOM to be fully loaded
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  // Get the input elements
  const chatNameInput = document.getElementById('chat-name');
  const firstNameInput = document.getElementById('chat-first-name');
  const surnameInput = document.getElementById('chat-surname-name'); // Corrected ID based on your HTML

  // Add an event listener to the chat-name input field
  chatNameInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
    // Get the value from the chat-name input and trim any extra whitespace
    const fullName = chatNameInput.value.trim();
    
    // Split the full name into an array of words
    const nameParts = fullName.split(' ');
    
    // Get the first name (the first element of the array)
    const firstName = nameParts[0];
    
    // Initialize surname
    let surname = '';
    
    // Check the number of name parts
    if (nameParts.length > 1) {
      // If there are more than one part, join all parts from the second one onwards
      surname = nameParts.slice(1).join(' ');
    } else {
      // If there is only one part (or the input is empty after the first word)
      surname = 'UNKNOWN';
    }
    
    // Update the first name and surname input fields
    firstNameInput.value = firstName;
    surnameInput.value = surname;
  });
});
</script></div><div class="code-set-chat-form-type w-embed w-script"><script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const input = document.getElementById('chat-form-type');
  if (!input) return;

  const setVal = (v) => {
    input.value = v;
    input.setAttribute('value', v); // keep attribute in sync for any readers
  };

  // DIV: #telegram-button → "telegram" (capture = true to run before other click handlers)
  const telegramDiv = document.getElementById('telegram-button');
  if (telegramDiv) {
    telegramDiv.addEventListener('click', () => setVal('telegram'), true);
  }

  // BUTTON: #contact-pop-button → "text-message" (only on real user clicks)
  const contactBtn = document.getElementById('contact-pop-button');
  if (contactBtn) {
    contactBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (!e.isTrusted) return; // ignore programmatic clicks
      setVal('text-message');
    }, true);
  }
});
</script></div></div></div></div><footer class="footer1_component"><div class="mint-banner"><div class="trusted-mint-logos"><div class="mint-logos-ticker"><img <script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    // Determine the environment value once
    const environmentValue = location.hostname.includes('webflow.io') ? 'STAGING' : 'PRODUCTION';

    // List of input IDs that need to be updated
    const inputIdsToUpdate =[
        'environment', 
        'chat-environment', 
        'environment-deals-promo', 
        'environment-gold-app', 
        'environment-form-footer', 
        'out-stock-environment'
    ];

    // Iterate over the list of IDs and update the corresponding input fields
    inputIdsToUpdate.forEach(id => {
        const inputField = document.getElementById(id);
        // Only update if the field exists on the current page. Do nothing if it's missing.
        if (inputField) {
            inputField.value = environmentValue;
        }
    });
});
</script>
<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  // Attach sanitizers to any input with id="mobile"
  const attachMobileSanitizer = (el) => {
    if (el.__mobileSanitized) return;
    const sanitize = () => {
      const start = el.selectionStart;
      const before = typeof start === 'number' ? el.value.slice(0, start) : '';
      const spacesBefore = (before.match(/\s/g) || []).length;

      const newVal = el.value.replace(/\s+/g, ''); // remove ALL whitespace
      if (el.value !== newVal) {
        el.value = newVal;
        // keep cursor position stable if possible
        if (typeof start === 'number') {
          const newPos = Math.max(0, start - spacesBefore);
          el.setSelectionRange(newPos, newPos);
        }
      }
    };

    ['input', 'change', 'blur', 'paste'].forEach(evt =>
      el.addEventListener(evt, sanitize)
    );

    el.__mobileSanitized = true;
  };

  // Initial pass (supports duplicate IDs across forms)
  document.querySelectorAll('input#mobile').forEach(attachMobileSanitizer);

  // Observe DOM changes (Webflow can inject forms dynamically)
  const obs = new MutationObserver(() => {
    document.querySelectorAll('input#mobile').forEach(attachMobileSanitizer);
  });
  obs.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });

  // Final safety: sanitize right before any form submission
  document.querySelectorAll('form').forEach(form => {
    form.addEventListener('submit', () => {
      form.querySelectorAll('input#mobile').forEach(el => {
        el.value = el.value.replace(/\s+/g, '');
      });
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
// Minimal setup with visibility detection
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  const tickerContainer = document.querySelector('.mint-logos-ticker');
  if (!tickerContainer) return;

  const images = tickerContainer.querySelectorAll('.logo-image, .logo-image-long, .logo-image-large');
  if (!images.length) return;

  // One-time setup
  const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
  wrapper.className = 'ticker-wrapper';
  images.forEach(image => wrapper.appendChild(image));
  
  const clone = wrapper.cloneNode(true);
  clone.className += ' ticker-clone';
  
  tickerContainer.innerHTML = '';
  tickerContainer.appendChild(wrapper);
  tickerContainer.appendChild(clone);

  // Visibility observer (lightweight)
  const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
    tickerContainer.classList.toggle('ticker-running', entries[0].isIntersecting);
  }, { threshold: 0.1 });

  observer.observe(tickerContainer);
});
</script>

<script>

//----------------------------------------------------
/**
 * Ultra-lightweight navbar controller - loads only when needed
 */

(function() {
  if (window.innerWidth < 768) return; // Skip mobile
  
  let navbar, heroSection, lastScrollY = window.scrollY, isFixed = true, heroBottom = 0, init = false;
  
  function scroll() {
    if (!init) {
      navbar = document.getElementById('home-navbar');
      heroSection = document.getElementById('hero-section');
      if (!navbar || !heroSection) return;
      heroBottom = heroSection.offsetTop + heroSection.offsetHeight;
      init = true;
    }
    
    const y = window.scrollY;
    const down = y > lastScrollY;
    
    if (down && y >= heroBottom && isFixed) {
      navbar.style.position = 'absolute';
      navbar.style.top = heroBottom + 'px';
      isFixed = false;
    } else if (!down && !isFixed) {
      navbar.style.position = 'fixed';
      navbar.style.top = '0';
      isFixed = true;
    }
    
    lastScrollY = y;
  }
  
  let tick = false;
  window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
    if (!tick) {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => { scroll(); tick = false; });
      tick = true;
    }
  }, { passive: true });
})();
</script>

<style>
  #home-navbar { transition: position 0.3s ease; }
</style>






<script>

// -------page display logic---------------------------------
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    // Find the element with ID 'spot-price'
    const spotPriceElement = document.getElementById('spot-price');
    
    if (spotPriceElement) {
        // Make it clickable
        spotPriceElement.style.cursor = 'pointer';
        
        // Add click event listener
        spotPriceElement.addEventListener('click', function() {
            // Get current domain and navigate to gold-price-nz page
            window.location.href = window.location.origin + '/live-prices/gold-price-nz';
        });
        
        // Optional: Add hover effect to make it look more like a link
        spotPriceElement.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
            this.style.opacity = '0.8';
        });
        
        spotPriceElement.addEventListener('mouseleave', function() {
            this.style.opacity = '1';
        });
    }

    // Add click logic for CMS button by ID and slug
    const cmsButtonId = '1-4-oz-gold-year-of-the-snake-2025';
    const cmsSlug = '1-4-oz-gold-year-of-the-snake-2025';
    const cmsBasePath = '/buy-gold-details/';
    
    const cmsButton = document.getElementById(cmsButtonId);
    
    if (cmsButton) {
        // Make it clickable
        cmsButton.style.cursor = 'pointer';
        
        // Add click event listener to navigate to correct CMS item URL
        cmsButton.addEventListener('click', function() {
            window.location.href = window.location.origin + cmsBasePath + cmsSlug;
        });
    }
});
</script>





<script>
//-------------------CUSTOMER REVIEWS LOGIC---------------------
function initUserReviews() {
    console.log("Homepage user reviews script started.");

    const reviewBlocks = document.querySelectorAll('.user-review-block');
    console.log(`Found ${reviewBlocks.length} review blocks on the homepage.`);

    reviewBlocks.forEach((block, index) => {
        const hiddenData = block.querySelector('.hidden-user-review-data');
        if (hiddenData) {
            const stars = parseInt(hiddenData.getAttribute('data-stars'), 10);
            const starsWrapper = block.querySelector('.stars-wrapper');
            if (starsWrapper) {
                const starElements = starsWrapper.querySelectorAll('.icon-embed-xxsmall');
                starElements.forEach((star, i) => {
                    star.style.display = (i < stars) ? 'block' : 'none';
                });
            }
        } else {
            console.log(`Hidden data not found for review block ${index}.`);
        }
    });
}

const observer = new MutationObserver((mutations, obs) => {
    const reviewContainer = document.querySelector('.user-reviews-cms-home');
    if (reviewContainer && reviewContainer.querySelector('.user-review-block')) {
        initUserReviews();
        obs.disconnect(); 
    }
});

observer.observe(document.body, {
    childList: true,
    subtree: true
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const reviewContainer = document.querySelector('.user-reviews-cms-home');
    if (reviewContainer && reviewContainer.querySelector('.user-review-block')) {
        initUserReviews();
    }
});
</script>
